        trace::mark("write_start");
        latency::finished(writer.write_all(&encoded).and_then(|_| writer.flush()).is_err() || refused);
    }
}

/// A 204 has no representation metadata or advertised bytes. Keeping it out
/// of the JSON response helper also prevents an unread `null` from framing the
/// next response on a persistent connection.
pub(crate) fn respond_empty(request: Request, status: u16) {
    let mut response = Response::empty(status);
    if let Some(header)=generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()).filter(|header| !header.field.equiv("Content-Type")) {
        response.add_header(header);
    }
    let _ = request.respond(response);
}

pub(crate) fn refusal_json(refusal: &Refusal) -> Json {
    serde_json::to_value(refusal).unwrap_or(Json::Null)
}


pub(crate) fn query_params(url: &str) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    fn decode(text: &str) -> String {
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(text.len());
        let bytes = text.as_bytes();
        let mut at = 0;
        while at < bytes.len() {
            if bytes[at] == b'%' && at + 2 < bytes.len() {
                let hex = |b: u8| (b as char).to_digit(16).map(|n| n as u8);
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (hex(bytes[at+1]), hex(bytes[at+2])) { out.push(a * 16 + b); at += 3; continue; }
            }
            out.push(if bytes[at] == b'+' { b' ' } else { bytes[at] }); at += 1;
        }
        String::from_utf8(out).unwrap_or_else(|_| text.to_owned())
    }
    url.split_once('?')
        .map(|(_, query)| query.split('&').filter_map(|pair| pair.split_once('=').map(|(k, v)| (decode(k), decode(v)))).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

// Identity and head are a single snapshot from the owner of the evaluated
// operation. Mutation replay's seq is historical; head is never that receipt.
fn response_head(engine: &mut Engine) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
    let id: String = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::from)?;
    Ok((id, tx.next_seq()? - 1))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a replay belongs to its acknowledged store, even if
// the endpoint changes between the client's probe and this transaction.
fn intent_store(engine: &Engine, payload: &Json) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(expected) = payload.get("store_id").filter(|id| !id.is_null()) else { return Ok(()); };
    let current = engine.store().meta("store_id")?;
    if expected.as_str().is_some_and(|id| Some(id) == current.as_deref()) { return Ok(()); }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the intent belongs to another store; its original outcome is unknown")
        .site("request.store_id").rewrite("reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent").guide("sync"))
}

fn operation_refusal(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, why: Refusal) {
    let metadata = shared.read_identity();
    match metadata {
        Ok((store_id, head, generation)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why), "store_id": store_id, "head": head, "generation": generation })),
        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why) })),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a racing id conflict is a terminal input refusal, not a transport failure.
fn mutation_error_status(refusal: &Refusal) -> u16 {
    if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if matches!(refusal.code.as_str(), "E_ID_REUSED" | "E_DELIVERY_BINDING") { 200 } else { 500 }
}

fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — lookup, decode and execution share the admitted release.
    let operation = request.url().split('?').next().is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("/q/") || p.starts_with("/m/"));
    let _scope=crate::release_requests::Scope::begin(*lock(&shared.generation));

    request.set_cleanup_guard(latency::FinishOnDrop);
    #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.request_work);
    let method = request.method().clone();
    let url = request.url().to_owned();
    let path = url.split('?').next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::begin(shared, &path);
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — closed read allow-list precedes every handler,
    // declared route, dev door and idempotency replay. Refusals carry no head.
    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.
    let public_door = path.starts_with("/dev/") || matches!(path.as_str(), "/seed" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/login" | "/auth/guest")
        || path == token::ENDPOINT || routes::declared(shared, &url).is_some();
    if request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("authorization")
        && (public_door || crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str()).is_none_or(|token| token.starts_with("ro.")))) {
        match principal_of(&request, shared) {
            Ok((_, _, snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read)) if !read_permitted(&method, &path) =>
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_READ_ONLY", "auth", "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes").guide("read-only")})),
            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — the private-proof door keeps one indistinguishable refusal.
            Err(_) if path == token::ENDPOINT => return token::denied(request),
            Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable {503} else {401}, json!({"denied": refusal_json(&why)})),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    if path == token::ENDPOINT { return token::handle(request, shared); }
    // This exact declared POST path is the only non-loopback development
    // exception. Core doors, mismatched methods and query variants stay fenced.
    if method == Method::Post && let Some((name, route)) = routes::declared(shared, &url) {
        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
    }
    let ephemeral_request = method == Method::Post && path.strip_prefix("/m/").is_some_and(|op| lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations.get(op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral));
    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
    };
    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
        return;
    }
    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
    if (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth",
            "development doors are closed; use serve staging with real users (guide deploy), or explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors for local fixtures").guide("deploy"))}));
    }
    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
    }
    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
    if method==Method::Post && matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") {
        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
            Ok(None)=>{},
            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),
        }
    }
    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "fixture catalog requires dev"))})); }
        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
        let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();
        drop(bound);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "tables": tables }));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.
        let generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
        let status = lock(&shared.adoption);
        let now = shared.now();
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Options {
        respond_empty(request, 204);
        return;
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    }
    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
    }
    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow
    // upload cannot retain the authority held at its start.
    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside
    // the SQLite writer transaction that closes the live session family.
    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")
        || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && path.ends_with("/cancel"))
        || matches!(path.as_str(), "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout-all")) {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            // No authenticated viewer: metadata cannot disclose a store head.
            if path.starts_with("/q/") { return respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
use super::*;

/// Who is asking: a bearer session, or (in `dev`, from loopback) a persona.
pub(crate) fn principal_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    principal_with_owner(request, shared, None)
}

fn principal_with_owner(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, owner: Option<&Engine>) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    // Declared public bearer routes keep their existing development admission.
    if !(request.method() == &Method::Post && routes::declared(shared, request.url()).is_some()
        && !request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona"))) {
        dev_request(request, shared)?;
    }
    let mut persona = None;
    let mut bearer = None;
    for header in request.headers() {
        let name = header.field.as_str().as_str().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let value = header.value.as_str().trim();
        if name == "x-snapback-persona" {
            if persona.is_some()
                || value.is_empty()
                || !value.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
            {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one valid x-snapback-persona header").guide("personas"));
            }
            persona = Some(value.to_owned());
        }
        if name == "authorization" {
            let Some(token) = crate::auth::parse_bearer(value) else {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send Authorization: Bearer <token>").guide("personas"));
            };
            if bearer.replace(token.to_owned()).is_some() {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one Authorization header").guide("personas"));
            }
        }
    }
    if persona.is_some() && bearer.is_some() {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send one credential: a development persona or a bearer token").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(persona) = persona {
        if shared.dev_doors() {
            // `use personas` is the cast: a test expecting an outsider to be
            // refused must not see it signed in. No declaration admits none.
            // (Taken briefly; the engine
            // guard, when held, is always acquired before this one.)
            let declared = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
            if !declared.contains(&persona) {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", format!(
                    "persona {persona} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast. Add {persona} to it (dev re-adopts on save) or send a declared persona", declared.join(", ")))
                    .rewrite(format!("use personas {}, {persona}", declared.join(", "))).guide("personas"));
            }
            return Ok((persona_principal(&persona), "persona".into(), snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full));
        }
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "personas are a development door; a deployed server takes bearer tokens (snapback4/auth)").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(token) = bearer {
        // Reads may authenticate against the committed generation during adoption.
        // Keep write authentication behind the writer: time queued for adoption
        // must not preserve a session that expired or was revoked in that wait.
        let path = request.url().split('?').next().unwrap_or("");
        let read = detached_request(request.method(), path);
        let resolve = |engine: &Engine, identity: Option<&str>, detached: bool| {
            let now = shared.now();
            let sample = snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now, supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) };
            let result = if detached {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample, identity)
            } else {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample)
            };
            let result=result.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|value|value.ok_or_else(||
                snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, now, trusted_dev_request(request, shared))));
            if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
            result
        };
        return if let Some(engine) = owner { resolve(engine, None, false) }
        else if read { match shared.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, reader)) => {
                let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
                let result = resolve(&reader, identity.as_deref(), true);
                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                result
            }
            Err(_) if shared.memory_store() => resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false),
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        } } else { resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false) };
    }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "sign in: send a bearer token (snapback4/auth), or x-snapback-persona in development").guide("personas"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — adoption's writer queue cannot retain expired bearer authority.
pub(super) fn mutation_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine, principal: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if principal_with_owner(request, shared, Some(engine))?.0 == principal { Ok(()) }
    else { Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the request's session changed; sign in again").guide("personas")) }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — additions require an explicit decision here.
pub(super) fn read_permitted(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
    match method {
        Method::Get => matches!(path, "/changes" | "/probe" | "/schema") || path.starts_with("/assets/"),
        Method::Post => matches!(path, "/sync" | "/probe" | "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout") || path.starts_with("/q/"),
        _ => false,
    }
}
//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Status {
                after,
                items_after,
                event,
            },
        ),
    }
}
fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let final_dir=loop {
                n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
                let path=releases.join(n.to_string());
                match std::fs::create_dir(&path) {Ok(())=>break path,Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists=>continue,Err(e)=>return Err(e.to_string())}
            };

//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let final_dir=loop {
                n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
                let path=releases.join(n.to_string());
                match std::fs::create_dir(&path) {Ok(())=>break path,Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists=>continue,Err(e)=>return Err(e.to_string())}
            };

//! Development doors beside the operation routes: `POST /seed`, `POST /dev/reset`, and the
//! `--no-watch` hint that names a schema edited on disk after adoption.
use super::{Shared, lock, refusal_json, respond_timed};
use crate::project::{Project, SourceStamp};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use snapback4_core::wire;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use tiny_http::Request;

/// The source stamp each `--no-watch` dev host adopted, by project root.
static PINNED: Mutex<BTreeMap<PathBuf, SourceStamp>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());

pub(super) fn pin(project: &Path, stamp: SourceStamp) {
    PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).insert(project.to_owned(), stamp);
}

/// A `--no-watch` host serves the schema it started with. When a request
/// names an operation or argument that host does not know and the `.q` files
/// changed on disk since, say so: the edit is real, it was just never adopted.
pub(super) fn stale(shared: &Shared, refusal: Refusal) -> Refusal {
    let Some(adopted) = PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get(&shared.project).copied() else { return refusal };
    if (Project { root: shared.project.clone() }).sources_stamp() == adopted { return refusal; }
    let message = format!("{}; this dev host runs with --no-watch and snapback/ changed on disk after it adopted its schema, so the edit is not served", refusal.message);
    Refusal { message, rewrite: Some("restart `snapback4 dev` (or drop --no-watch so saves re-adopt), then retry".into()), ..refusal }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — persona names are not their principals; dev only.
pub(super) fn persona_argument(shared: &Shared, kinds: &BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &Json) -> Option<Refusal> {
    if !shared.personas { return None; }
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    crate::persona_principals::arguments(&bound.personas,kinds,args)
}
// Rows are written as the system principal; each names its id because seed is an upsert by id.
pub(super) fn seed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
        return;
    }
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("personas")) }));
    let rows: Vec<Json> = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(rows)) => rows,
        _ => return refuse(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "seed takes a JSON array of {table, row}")),
    };
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    let now = shared.now();
    let typed = match typed_rows(engine.schema(), &rows, now) {
        Ok(typed) => typed,
        Err(refusal) => { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    };
    let outcome = engine.seed_at(typed, now);
    drop(engine);
    match outcome {
        Ok(count) => {
            shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "seeded": count }));
        }
        Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&seed_refusal(error)) })),
    }
}

/// `/seed` rows checked against the served schema and typed, or the first problem.
fn typed_rows(schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, rows: &[Json], now: i64) -> Result<Vec<(String, snapback4_core::Value)>, Refusal> {
    let mut typed = Vec::new();
    for (index, entry) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let (Some(table), Some(row)) = (entry.get("table").and_then(Json::as_str), entry.get("row")) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "each seed entry is {table, row}"));
        };
        let Some(definition) = schema.table(table) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("unknown table {table}")));
        };
        if row.is_object() && row.get("id").is_none_or(|id| !id.is_string()) {
            return Err(missing_id(index, table));
        }
        let mut row = row.clone();
        crate::verbs::test_fixture::relative_times(definition, &mut row, now)
            .map_err(|why| Refusal::new("E_FIXTURE_TIME", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")))?;
        if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &row) {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")));
        }
        match wire::row_from_json(definition, &row) {
            Ok(value) => typed.push((table.to_owned(), value)),
            Err(why) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{table}: {why}"))),
        }
    }
    Ok(typed)
}

fn seed_refusal(error: snapback4_core::RuntimeError) -> Refusal {
    let mut refusal = error.refusal();
    // Reseeding with changed immutable values reads like a rule problem; say what happened.
    if refusal.code == "E_CONSTRAINT" && refusal.message.ends_with(" is immutable") {
        refusal.message.push_str("; /seed is an upsert by id, and a row with that id already exists with a different value in that column. Reseed it with its original value, give the row a new id, POST /dev/reset first, or restart dev with --memory for an empty store");
    }
    refusal
}

/// What `POST /dev/reset` was asked to do: the body is empty, a `/seed`
/// array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}` with either key optional.
#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
struct ResetRequest { seed: Vec<Json>, clock: bool }

fn reset_request(body: &str) -> Result<ResetRequest, Refusal> {
    const SHAPE: &str = "reset takes no body, a /seed array [{table, row}, ..], or {\"seed\": [..], \"clock\": \"reset\"} with either key optional";
    if body.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(ResetRequest::default()); }
    match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(seed)) => Ok(ResetRequest { seed, clock: false }),
        Ok(Json::Object(fields)) => {
            let mut request = ResetRequest::default();
            for (key, value) in fields {
                match (key.as_str(), value) {
                    ("seed", Json::Array(seed)) => request.seed = seed,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "reset" => request.clock = true,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "keep" => request.clock = false,
                    ("clock", other) => return Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("reset's clock is \"reset\" (back to wall time) or \"keep\" (the default), not {other}"))),
                    (key, _) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{SHAPE}; got key {key:?}"))),
                }
            }
            Ok(request)
        }
        _ => Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", SHAPE)),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — one transaction: empty, new identity, reseed; then the host's caches follow.
pub(super) fn reset(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&reset_release()) }));
        return;
    }
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("testing")) }));
    let asked = match reset_request(body) {
        Ok(asked) => asked,
        Err(refusal) => return refuse(request, refusal),
    };
    // Serialize with clock advances, then take the engine before the clock:
    // the order clock advance and worker claims use.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock).clone();
    if asked.clock && let Err(refusal) = clock.reset() { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    let now = clock.now();
    // Resolve against the prospective reset clock before changing any durable state.
    let typed = match typed_rows(engine.schema(), &asked.seed, now) {
        Ok(typed) => typed,
        Err(refusal) => { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    };
    let reset = match engine.dev_reset_at(typed, now) {
        Ok(reset) => reset,
        Err(error) => {
            drop(engine);
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&seed_refusal(error)) }));
        }
    };
    if asked.clock { *lock(&shared.clock) = clock; }
    // The reset committed a new identity and a new authority clock. Replace,
    // rather than max with, the retired store's cached high-water mark.
    shared.time_audience_clock.store(if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|t| t.not_before.is_some()) { now } else { i64::MIN }, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release);
    // Detached readers reopen; the following registry, change answers and
    // `/dev/status` forget the old identity. Wake then names the new store,
    // so every held poll returns and each client sees the replacement.
    let sites = lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone();
    lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &sites);
    lock(&shared.adoption).store_id = reset.store_id.clone();
    if let Ok(reader) = snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new() { *lock(&shared.following) = reader; }
    *lock(&shared.change_answers) = Default::default();
    shared.durable_committed(&engine);
    let generation = engine.generation();
    drop(engine);
    let assets = reset.assets.len();
    if let Err(error) = reset.assets.remove_files() { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: reset left asset bytes behind: {error}"); }
    let now = shared.now();
    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: store reset (store {} → {}, seeded {}) in {} ms", reset.previous_store_id, reset.store_id, reset.seeded, started.elapsed().as_millis());
    respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({
        "reset": true, "seeded": reset.seeded, "generation": generation, "storeId": reset.store_id,
        "previousStoreId": reset.previous_store_id, "head": reset.seq, "assetsRemoved": assets,
        "now": now, "clockOffsetMs": now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "skewMs": now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "ms": started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
    }));
}

pub(super) fn reset_release() -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "resetting the store is a development door; snapback4 serve never empties a store")
        .guide("testing")
}

fn missing_id(index: usize, table: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_FIELD", "input", format!(
        "missing field id: seed row {index} ({table}) has no string id. /seed is an upsert by id, so every row names its own; \
         rerunning the same seed then updates those rows instead of duplicating them"))
        .rewrite("give each row a 26-character id in Crockford base32 (digits and capital letters except I, L, O, U), \
                  e.g. \"row\": {\"id\": \"0000000000000000000000SEED\", ..}; in JavaScript `idFrom(\"seed:alice\")` from \
                  snapback4/client derives the same valid id from a label every time, and a reference column takes that id")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn the_missing_id_example_is_a_valid_id() {
        let refusal = super::missing_id(0, "notes");
        let rewrite = refusal.rewrite.unwrap();
        let example = rewrite.split("\"id\": \"").nth(1).unwrap().split('"').next().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(example.len(), 26, "{example}");

                        retry = retryable(&error);
                    }

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — direct children, file logs and bounded cleanup own proof orchestration.
import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { closeSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { stripVTControlCharacters } from "node:util";

const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
process.once("exit", () => { for (const child of children) child.kill("SIGKILL"); });
export function register(child: ChildProcess): void {
  children.add(child);
  child.once("exit", () => children.delete(child));
  child.on("error", () => children.delete(child));
}
export interface ProofOptions {
  cwd?: string;
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  args?: readonly string[];
  timeoutMs?: number;
  stopTimeoutMs?: number;
  logsDir?: string;
  /** Keep stdout in memory only (stderr remains diagnostic evidence). */
  secret?: boolean;
  /** Internal: a frontend keeper owns this private process group. */
  detached?: boolean;
  /** Internal: the frontend observes parent death through stdin EOF. */
  livenessPipe?: boolean;
}
export interface ProofHost {
  url: string;
  pid: number;
  logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string };
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  const value = ms ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");
  return value;
}
export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    })]);
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
  const errors: unknown[] = [];
  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
  }
  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
}
export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 5000, "host stop"); return;
  }
  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
  }
  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
  let done!: () => void;
  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
  child.once("exit", done);
  child.once("error", done);
  try {
    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    catch {
      child.kill("SIGKILL");
      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    }
  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
}
export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)
      || Number(low) < 1 || Number(high) > 65535 || Number(low) > Number(high)) {
    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
  }
  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
}
/** App-local evidence survives harness cleanup of its temporary root. */
export function logsDir(options: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> = {}): string {
  return resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", options.logsDir ?? "logs");
}
export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
  let child: ChildProcess;
  try { child = spawn(command, [...args], { cwd, detached: options.detached ?? false, env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err] }); }
  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
  let stdout = "";
  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
  register(child);
  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
  let error: Error | undefined;
  child.on("error", value => { error = value; });
  const closed = new Promise<void>(resolve => child.once("close", () => resolve()));
  return { child, logs, id, closed, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
}
export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
    }
    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
      const text = logText(path);
      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
        if (url) return url;
      }
      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
    }
    await sleep(25);
  }
  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
}

    const reset = await fetch(dev.url + "/dev/reset", { method: "POST", body: "{}" }).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.ok(reset.now >= advanced.now); assert.ok(reset.skewMs >= 7_199_000);
    assert.ok(await dev.now() >= reset.now);
    web = await startStatic("dist", { cwd, env }); assert.notEqual(web.url, dev.url);
    assert.equal(await (await fetch(web.url)).text(), "<h1>owned</h1>");
    for (const log of Object.values(dev.logs)) assert.ok(log.startsWith(join(cwd, "logs")), "default logs survive temp-root harness cleanup");
    assert.match(readFileSync(dev.logs.stderr, "utf8"), /SNAPBACK4_READY/);
  } finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => stopChild(dev)); }
});

test("ANSI and blank ready lines, early exit, stalled readiness and TERM escalation", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  const run = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "console.log(''); console.log('\\x1b[32mREADY http://127.0.0.1:1234\\x1b[0m'); setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], { cwd });
  assert.equal(await ready(run, line => line.startsWith("READY ") ? line.slice(6) : undefined, 3000), "http://127.0.0.1:1234"); await stopChild(run.child);
  const dead = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "process.exit(2)"], { cwd }); await assert.rejects(ready(dead, () => undefined, 3000), /before ready/);
  const stall = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "process.on('SIGTERM',()=>{}); console.log('ready'); setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], { cwd });
  await ready(stall, line => line === "ready" ? "ready" : undefined, 3000);
  await assert.rejects(ready(stall, () => undefined, 30), /timed out/); await stopChild(stall.child, 30); assert.equal(stall.child.signalCode, "SIGKILL");
});

test("exit sweep kills registered child, not a neighboring child", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t), pidFile = join(cwd, "child.pid");
  const helper = fileURLToPath(new URL("../dist/proof-process.js", import.meta.url));
  const script = `import {logged, ready} from ${JSON.stringify(helper)}; import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs'; const run=logged(process.execPath,['-e',"console.log('ready'); setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], {cwd:${JSON.stringify(cwd)}}); await ready(run,l=>l==='ready'?'ok':undefined,5000); writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(pidFile)}, String(run.child.pid)); process.exit(0);`;
  const parent = spawn(process.execPath, ["--input-type=module", "-e", script], { stdio: "ignore" }); await new Promise(resolve => parent.once("close", resolve));
  const pid = Number(readFileSync(pidFile, "utf8"));
  for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) {
    try { process.kill(pid, 0); if (process.platform === "linux" && /\) Z /.test(readFileSync(`/proc/${pid}/stat`, "utf8"))) return; }
    catch { return; }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
  }
  assert.fail(`child ${pid} survived proof exit`);
});

test("SIGKILLed driver closes frontend liveness and sweeps its whole private group", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t); mkdirSync(join(cwd, "dist")); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "dist/index.html"), "owned");
  const pidFile = join(cwd, "frontend.pid");
  const module = fileURLToPath(new URL("../dist/proof.js", import.meta.url));
  const parent = spawn(process.execPath, ["--input-type=module", "-e", `import {startStatic} from ${JSON.stringify(module)}; import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs'; const host=await startStatic('dist',{cwd:${JSON.stringify(cwd)}}); writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(pidFile)},String(host.pid));`], { stdio: "ignore" });
  t.after(() => parent.kill("SIGKILL"));
  await bounded((async () => { while (true) { try { readFileSync(pidFile); return; } catch {} await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20)); } })(), 5000, "frontend start");
  const pid = Number(readFileSync(pidFile, "utf8"));
  parent.kill("SIGKILL"); await new Promise(resolve => parent.once("close", resolve));
  // Only inspect. Signals are dispatched by the live keeper to its own group.
  const { execFileSync } = await import("node:child_process");
  await bounded((async () => {
    while (true) {
      const rows = execFileSync("ps", ["-eo", "pgid=,stat="], { encoding: "utf8", timeout: 2000 });
      const living = rows.trim().split("\n").some(row => { const [group, state] = row.trim().split(/\s+/); return Number(group) === pid && !state.startsWith("Z"); });
      if (!living) return;
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
    }
  })(), 5000, "frontend group drain");
});

test("secret capture releases its pipe when an exited tool leaves a descendant holding stdout", {
  skip: process.platform === "win32" && "The pipe-holder exit witness uses portable Unix ps (Linux and Darwin)",
}, async t => {
  const cwd=fixture(t),module=fileURLToPath(new URL('../dist/proof.js',import.meta.url));
  const script=join(cwd,'driver.mjs'),marker=join(cwd,'desc-ready');
  writeFileSync(script, `import {command} from ${JSON.stringify(module)};
try {await command(process.execPath,['-e',\`const child=require('node:child_process').spawn(process.execPath,['-e','setTimeout(()=>{},6000)'],{stdio:['ignore',1,2]});require('node:fs').writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(marker)},JSON.stringify({pid:child.pid}));process.exit(0)\`],{cwd:${JSON.stringify(cwd)},secret:true,timeoutMs:1000});process.exitCode=1;}
catch(error){if(!/timed out/.test(error.message))throw error;}`);
  const child=spawn(process.execPath,[script],{stdio:'ignore'});
  let holder;
  const closed=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{child.once('error',reject);child.once('close',resolve)});
  t.after(async()=>{
    if(child.exitCode===null&&child.signalCode===null)child.kill('SIGKILL');
    // The regression deliberately outlives the driver. Observe its own finite
    // lifetime before ending the file; never signal a retained descendant PID.
    if(holder) await bounded((async()=>{
      while(true){
        let state;
        try {state=execFileSync('ps',['-p',String(holder),'-o','stat='],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:2000}).trim();}
        catch(error){if(error.status===1 && !String(error.stdout).trim())return;throw error;}
        if(state.startsWith('Z'))return;
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,50));
      }
    })(),10000,'pipe holder exit');
  });
  await bounded((async()=>{while(!holder){try{holder=JSON.parse(readFileSync(marker,'utf8')).pid;}catch(error){if(error.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}if(!holder)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,20));}})(),5000,'pipe holder spawned');
  assert.equal(await bounded(closed,5000,'secret driver exit'),0);
});
