//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, producing_engine, respond_with_cors};
use snapback4_core::{error::Refusal, identity::{self, Session}};
use std::{io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, net::TcpStream, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use tiny_http::{Method, Request};

pub(crate) const ENDPOINT: &str = "/dev/token";
#[path = "serve_token_protocol.rs"]
mod protocol;
use protocol::{Mint, CLIENT, SERVER, PROOF};
pub(crate) use protocol::Authority;
const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;
const SECRET_LEN: usize = 64;

fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    path.push("-token");
    path.into()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        file.set_len(0)?;
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
        }
        let mut secret = String::new();
        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
        }
        Ok(secret)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
}

pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
        },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let Some(server) = server else {
        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
    };
    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let result = {
        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, snapback4_core::now_ms(), mint.days)
        })
    };
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
        Err(error) => {
            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
            json!({"denied": why})
        }
    };
    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
}

fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
    let mut response = String::new();
    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
    }
    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
        }
        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
    }
    let session = &reply["session"];
    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
    }
    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
}

//! Ownership locks the database's sidecar; stat pins its inode. `serve.lock` is only an address hint.
use std::{fs::{self, File}, io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError, Store};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;

pub(crate) struct Owner {
    _sidecar: std::sync::Arc<File>,
    identity: (u64, u64),
    path: PathBuf,
    record: Option<File>,
    reclaimed: Option<String>,
    pub(crate) token_authority: Option<crate::serve::token::Authority>,
}

/// The address `doctor` dials for the server that owns this store: the
/// published loopback address itself, or, for a wildcard bind, that family's
/// loopback on the same port. A published non-loopback address is not followed.
pub(crate) fn inspector_address(store: &Path) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().ok()?;
    dialable(&address(&path)?)
}

fn dialable(published: &str) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, Ipv6Addr, SocketAddr};
    let address = published.strip_prefix("http://")?.parse::<SocketAddr>().ok()?;
    let ip = match address.ip() {
        ip if ip.is_loopback() => ip,
        IpAddr::V4(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST),
        IpAddr::V6(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V6(Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST),
        _ => return None,
    };
    (address.port() != 0).then(|| SocketAddr::new(ip, address.port()))
}

fn resolve(store: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    match store.canonicalize() {
        Ok(path) => Ok(path),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            let parent = store.parent().filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty()).unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
            snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(parent).map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?;
            Ok(parent.canonicalize().map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?.join(store.file_name().ok_or("store path has no filename")?))
        }
        Err(e) => Err(failure(store, e)),
    }
}

fn failure(path: &Path, reason: impl std::fmt::Display) -> String {
    format!("E_STORE at {}: {reason}; restore a regular store/address file and retry", path.display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn same_file(a: &fs::Metadata, b: &fs::Metadata) -> bool { a.dev() == b.dev() && a.ino() == b.ino() }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn regular(path: &Path, create: bool, database: &Path) -> Result<File, String> {
    snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(database, path, create, 128)
        .map_err(|e| failure(path, e))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — sidecar flocks fence backend writers and share device ownership without locking SQLite.
pub(crate) fn command(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::ZERO) }
pub(crate) fn device(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, true, Duration::ZERO) }
// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.3 — a restarting host waits for watchdogs to finish group cleanup.
pub(crate) fn host(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::from_secs(5)) }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn acquire(store: &Path, shared: bool, wait: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + wait;
    for _ in 0..16 {
        let path = resolve(store)?;
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(path.parent().ok_or("store path has no parent")?)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        // Stat before opening either auxiliary file; a refused probe creates
        // no database. Only the sidecar lock grants permission to create one.
        let before = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let sidecar = snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_file(&path)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&path), e))?;
        let mut cleanup = None;
        loop {
            let locked = if shared { sidecar.try_lock_shared() } else { sidecar.try_lock() };
            match locked {
                Ok(()) => break,
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now() < deadline && *cleanup.get_or_insert_with(|| stopped_host(&path)) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => return Err(busy(&path)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::Error(error)) => return Err(failure(&path, error)),
            }
        }
        if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&path, &sidecar) { continue; }
        snapback4_core::private_fs::database_file(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let database = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?
            .ok_or_else(|| failure(&path, "database disappeared during ownership acquisition"))?;
        if before.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| !same_file(old, &database)) { continue; }
        let mut reclaimed = None;
        let record = if shared { None } else {
            let record_path = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let Some(pid)=owner_pid(&path) {
                if unsafe {libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t,0)} == -1 && std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error()==Some(libc::ESRCH) {
                    reclaimed = Some(format!("reclaimed stale serve.lock: dead PID {pid}; directory {}", path.parent().unwrap().display()));
                }
            }
            let file = regular(&record_path, true, &path)?;
            file.set_len(0).map_err(|e| failure(&record_path, e))?;
            Some(file)
        };
        return Ok(Owner { _sidecar: std::sync::Arc::new(sidecar), identity: (database.dev(), database.ino()), path, record, reclaimed, token_authority: None });
    }
    Err(failure(store, "database path kept changing during ownership acquisition"))
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn acquire(store: &Path, _: bool, _: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> { Err(failure(store, "database ownership requires Unix")) }

fn stopped_host(path: &Path) -> bool {
    // A CLI owner or a host not yet announced still refuses immediately. A
    // reachable listener does too; only a prior host's cleanup gets a wait.
    // This is a refusal/wait hint, never permission to open the store: the
    // sidecar must still be acquired. Never dial a non-loopback address hint.
    address(path).is_some_and(|published| dialable(&published).is_none_or(|address|
        std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_millis(50)).is_err()))
}

pub(crate) fn busy(path: &Path) -> String {
    let owner = address(path).map(|address| match owner_pid(path) { Some(pid) => format!("{address} (pid {pid})"), None => address })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: this store is live; owned by {owner}; directory {}; no work was admitted. Stop its `snapback4 dev` or `snapback4 serve` process (or wait for the owning CLI command), rerun this command once, then restart the server. Exclusion holds while <database>-owner is left in place; deleting or replacing it while a process runs ends exclusion and does not stop that process", path.display(),path.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn address(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    // A missing/replaced address record cannot grant ownership. Never follow
    // a symlink (including one to the database), nor block on a FIFO.
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    let mut lines = text.lines();
    let port = lines.next().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok());
    lines.next().map(str::to_owned).or_else(|| port.map(|p| format!("http://127.0.0.1:{p}")))
}

/// The owning host's PID from its address record (`pid N` on the third line).
#[cfg(unix)]
fn owner_pid(path: &Path) -> Option<u32> {
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    text.lines().nth(2)?.strip_prefix("pid ")?.parse().ok()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn owner_pid(_: &Path) -> Option<u32> { None }

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn address(_: &Path) -> Option<String> { None }

impl Owner {
    pub(crate) fn report_reclaim(&self, verbose: bool) {
        if verbose && let Some(note) = &self.reclaimed { eprintln!("note: {note}"); }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — unlink only our address inode; the ownership sidecar remains named.
    pub(crate) fn clear_address(&self) {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        if let Some(file) = &self.record {
            let path = self.path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let (Ok(held), Ok(named)) = (file.metadata(), fs::symlink_metadata(&path))
                && same_file(&held, &named) {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(path);
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn worker_lock(&self) -> std::sync::Arc<File> { self._sidecar.clone() }

//! Host startup; request scheduling remains in serve.rs.
use super::*;
pub fn serve(
    project: Project,
    port: u16,
    mut mode: Mode,
    bind: &str,
    inherited: Option<std::net::TcpListener>,
    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let capacity = capacity.fit()?;
    crate::host_limits::install(capacity);
    let mut source_stamp = project.sources_stamp();
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        let verbose = !matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: false, .. });
        let (reports, hidden) = crate::verbs::problem_reports(&bound, true, verbose);
        let shown = reports.len();
        for report in reports {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{report}");
        }
        if hidden > 0 {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 dev: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"
            );
        }
        return Err(format!(
            "{shown} problem{} in snapback/",
            if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
        ));
    }
    let dev = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { .. });
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — replay suppresses new effects and holds schedule admission; ordinary holds keep their separate meanings.
    let replay = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { replay: true, .. });
    let hold_effects = matches!(
        mode,
        Mode::Dev {
            hold_effects: true,
            ..
        }
    );
    let hold_schedules = replay
        || matches!(
            mode,
            Mode::Dev {
                hold_schedules: true,
                ..
            }
        );
    let clock = crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(
        dev,
        match mode {
            Mode::Dev { clock_offset, .. } => clock_offset,
            _ => 0,
        },
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let generated = if dev {
        Some(project.write_generated(&bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    if dev {
        project.check_server_at(&mut source_stamp)?;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — only the owning host may replace replay/schedule policy; deployment shares the same lock.
    // Release startup already owns this lock before selecting its source.
    let mut host_lock = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: false, .. }) {
        Some(crate::store_owner::host(&project.store_path())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    // A busy port is a failed start, not permission to change the stopped host's policy.
    let server =
        crate::witness_socket::server(bind, port, inherited, capacity.connections(), || {
            crate::listen_refusal::in_use(bind, port, if dev { "dev" } else { "serve" })
        })?;
    let address = server
        .server_addr()
        .to_ip()
        .ok_or("HTTP listener has no IP address")?;
    if let Some(file) = &mut host_lock {
        crate::store_owner::announce(file, address)?;
    }
    if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mut mode {
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — publish through the acquired handle, including a release database symlink.
        crate::store_owner::announce(
            Arc::get_mut(owner).ok_or("release owner already shared")?,
            address,
        )?;
    }
    let host_owner = match &mode {
        Mode::Release { owner, .. } => Some(owner.clone()),
        _ => host_lock.take().map(Arc::new),
    };
    if let Some(owner) = &host_owner { owner.report_reclaim(matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: true, .. })); }
    // `--fresh` has private, disposable storage and the same WAL readers as dev.
    let fresh = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. }) {
        Some(query_readers::FreshDirectory::new()?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let mut engine = match &mode {
        Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. } => Engine::new_at(
            fresh.as_ref().unwrap().store().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
            bound.schema.clone(),
            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
            clock.now(),
        ),
        Mode::Dev { .. } => adoption::open(host_owner.as_deref().unwrap(), &bound, &clock),
        Mode::Release { expected, .. } => host_owner
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .open_store(Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|store| project.open_release_engine(store, &bound, *expected, clock.now())),
    }
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    adoption::report(&engine);
    if dev {

exec

test("runtime port range is inclusive, validated, and never cached", () => {
  assert.equal(portRange({}), "0"); assert.equal(portRange({ PORT_LO: "29040", PORT_HI: "29049" }), "29040-29049");
  for (const env of [{ PORT_LO: "2" }, { PORT_HI: "2" }, { PORT_LO: "3", PORT_HI: "2" }, { PORT_LO: "0", PORT_HI: "2" }, { PORT_LO: "2", PORT_HI: "65536" }, { PORT_LO: "x", PORT_HI: "3" }]) assert.throws(() => portRange(env), /PORT_LO/);
});
test("native and static bind beyond an occupied port; identity and independent logs", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t); mkdirSync(join(cwd, "dist")); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "dist/index.html"), "<h1>owned</h1>");
  const held = createServer(); const port = await bind(held); t.after(() => held.close());
  const env = { PORT_LO: String(port), PORT_HI: String(Math.min(65535, port + 20)) };
  const dev = await startDev({ cwd, env }); let web;
  try {
    assert.deepEqual(dev.personas, ["alice", "bob"]);
    assert.ok(Number(new URL(dev.url).port) > port); assert.equal((await (await fetch(dev.url + "/dev/status")).json()).pid, dev.pid);
    const before = await dev.now();
    const advanced = await fetch(dev.url + "/dev/clock/advance", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({duration:"2h"}) }).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.ok(advanced.now >= before + 7_200_000); assert.ok(Math.abs(advanced.skewMs - 7_200_000) < 1000);
    assert.ok(await dev.now() >= advanced.now);
    const reset = await fetch(dev.url + "/dev/reset", { method: "POST", body: "{}" }).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.ok(reset.now >= advanced.now); assert.ok(reset.skewMs >= 7_199_000);
    assert.ok(await dev.now() >= reset.now);
    web = await startStatic("dist", { cwd, env }); assert.notEqual(web.url, dev.url);
    assert.equal(await (await fetch(web.url)).text(), "<h1>owned</h1>");
    for (const log of Object.values(dev.logs)) assert.ok(log.startsWith(join(cwd, "logs")), "default logs survive temp-root harness cleanup");
    assert.match(readFileSync(dev.logs.stderr, "utf8"), /SNAPBACK4_READY/);
  } finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => stopChild(dev)); }
});

});

  const cwd = fixture(t); mkdirSync(join(cwd, "dist")); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "dist/index.html"), "shell");
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "dist/device.wasm"), "wasm"); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "outside.txt"), "secret");
  symlinkSync(join(cwd, "outside.txt"), join(cwd, "dist/leak.txt"));
  const web = await startStatic("dist", { cwd });
  try {
    assert.equal((await fetch(web.url + "/device.wasm")).headers.get("content-type"), "application/wasm");
    assert.equal((await fetch(web.url + "/leak.txt")).status, 403);
    assert.equal(await (await fetch(web.url + "/app/route")).text(), "shell");
    assert.equal((await fetch(web.url + "/missing.js")).status, 404);
  } finally { await web.stop(); }
});

  const cwd = fixture(t); mkdirSync(join(cwd, "versions")); mkdirSync(join(cwd, "fixtures"));

  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "snapback/schema.q"), "invalid current working source");

  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "versions/v1/schema.q"), schema);
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "snapback/client-compatibility.json"), JSON.stringify({ baseline: "versions/v1", since_release: 2 }));
  const one = await deploy("deploy", "versions/v1", { cwd }); assert.equal(one.code, 0);
  const preview = await cli(["deploy", "deploy", "--schema", v2, "--check"], { cwd }); assert.equal(preview.code, 0, preview.stderr);
  const serve = await startServe("deploy", { cwd });
  try {
    const identity = await (await fetch(serve.url + "/auth/config")).json(); assert.ok(identity.proof_id);

    assert.deepEqual(query.data, [], "saved file names never rename the schema's operations");

    assert.equal((await deploy("deploy", v2, { cwd })).code, 0);
    assert.equal((await rollback("deploy", 1, { cwd })).code, 0);
    assert.equal((await rollback("deploy", "latest", { cwd })).code, 0);
  } finally { await serve.stop(); }
  assert.equal(readFileSync(join(cwd, "snapback/schema.q"), "utf8"), "invalid current working source");

});
test("cli awaits close, separates output and returns refusal status", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  const result = await cli(["guide", "testing"], { cwd }); assert.equal(result.code, 0); assert.match(result.stdout, /Testing/);
  const refusal = await cli(["not-a-command"], { cwd }); assert.notEqual(refusal.code, 0); assert.ok(refusal.stderr);
});
test("ANSI and blank ready lines, early exit, stalled readiness and TERM escalation", async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  const run = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "console.log(''); console.log('\\x1b[32mREADY http://127.0.0.1:1234\\x1b[0m'); setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], { cwd });
  assert.equal(await ready(run, line => line.startsWith("READY ") ? line.slice(6) : undefined, 3000), "http://127.0.0.1:1234"); await stopChild(run.child);
  const dead = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "process.exit(2)"], { cwd }); await assert.rejects(ready(dead, () => undefined, 3000), /before ready/);
  const stall = logged(process.execPath, ["-e", "process.on('SIGTERM',()=>{}); console.log('ready'); setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], { cwd });
  await ready(stall, line => line === "ready" ? "ready" : undefined, 3000);
  await assert.rejects(ready(stall, () => undefined, 30), /timed out/); await stopChild(stall.child, 30); assert.equal(stall.child.signalCode, "SIGKILL");
});
test("cleanup bounds stalled actions and continues after errors", async () => {
  let later = false;
  await assert.rejects(cleanup(() => { throw Error("broken"); }, () => { later = true; }), AggregateError); assert.ok(later);
  await assert.rejects(bounded(new Promise(() => {}), 20, "stall"), /timed out/);
  await assert.rejects(waitForFresh({ count: () => new Promise(() => {}) }, null, 20), /no fresh signal/);
  await assert.rejects(stopChild({ nope: true }), /ChildProcess/);
});

  const parent = spawn(process.execPath, ["--input-type=module", "-e", script], { stdio: "ignore" }); await new Promise(resolve => parent.once("close", resolve));

#[test]
fn production_auth_refusals_use_http_auth_statuses_and_keep_the_body() {
    let host = Host::with_store(false, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),
        "use identity password\nquery who():\n  return viewer\nmutation ping():\n  return { ok: true }\n");
    for (route, payload) in [
        ("/auth/guest", "{}"),
        ("/auth/login", r#"{"email":"nobody@example.test","password":"wrong-password"}"#),
    ] {
        let response = host.request("POST", route, "", payload);
        assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "{response}");
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{response}");
    }
    let response = host.request("POST", "/seed", "", "[]");
    assert_eq!(status(&response), 403, "{response}");
    assert_eq!(status(&host.request("POST", "/q/who", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", "{}")), 401);
    let payload = r#"{"email":"ops@example.test","password":"private-test-password"}"#;
    let response = host.request("POST", "/auth/signup", "", payload);
    assert_eq!(status(&response), 200, "{response}");
    let token = body(&response)["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {token}\r\n");
    assert_eq!(status(&host.request("POST", "/auth/login", "", payload)), 200);
    let write = r#"{"id":"replay-test","args":{}}"#;
    let first = body(&host.request("POST", "/m/ping", &headers, write));
    assert_eq!(first["result"], json!({"ok":true}));
    let replay = body(&host.request("POST", "/m/ping", &headers, write));
    assert_eq!(replay["replayed"], true);
    assert!(replay.get("result").is_none(), "durable receipts do not retain historical results: {replay}");
    assert!(replay.get("current").is_none());
    let service = {
        let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        snapback4_core::identity::service_session(engine.store(), "ops", host.shared.now(), 1).unwrap()
    };
    let headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", service.token);
    for payload in [
        json!({"principal":"user:reserved"}),
        json!({"principal":"user:reserved", "binding":{"invocationId":"grant", "intent":"identity-session/v1"}}),
    ] {
        let response = host.request("POST", "/auth/session", &headers, &payload.to_string());
        assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "{response}");
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
    }
}
        assert_eq!(done_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap(), 2); adoption.join().unwrap();
        loop {
            let ran = engine.lock().unwrap().run_job_chunk(snapback4_core::now_ms()).unwrap();
            let Some(run) = ran else { break; };
            assert_ne!(run.state, snapback4_core::JobState::Parked, "{run:?}");
        }
        let engine = engine.lock().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("entries").unwrap().len(), 800);
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("repostEdges").unwrap().len(), 62);
    }
}

#[test]
fn development_banner_and_release_auth_boundary() {
    let dir = Directory::new();
    fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\nuse identity guests\ntable items:\n  body: text <=100\n  read <- allow\nquery all():\n  return items[] first 10 by byId\n").unwrap();
    let request = |port, path: &str, body: Value, credential: &str| -> (u16, Value) {
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).unwrap();
        let body = body.to_string();
        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\n{credential}Content-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len()).unwrap();
        let mut answer = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut answer).unwrap();
        let (head, body) = answer.split_once("\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
        (head.split_whitespace().nth(1).unwrap().parse().unwrap(), serde_json::from_str(body).unwrap())
    };
    let rows = json!([{"table":"items","row":{"id":"one","body":"development"}}]);
    let dev = Host::start(&dir, &["dev"]);
    assert_eq!(request(dev.port, "/seed", rows.clone(), ""), (200, json!({"seeded":1,"generation":1})));
    let (status, read) = request(dev.port, "/q/all", json!({"args":{}}), "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n");
    assert_eq!(status, 200); assert_eq!(read["data"][0]["body"], "development");
    let banner = dev.log();
    drop(dev);
    let deployment = dir.0.join("release");
    let result = dir.run(&["deploy", deployment.to_str().unwrap()]);
    assert!(result.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));
    let release = Host::start(&dir, &["serve", deployment.to_str().unwrap()]);
    let (status, seed) = request(release.port, "/seed", rows, "");
    assert_eq!(status, 403); assert_eq!(seed["denied"]["message"], "seeding is a development door");
    let (status, persona) = request(release.port, "/q/all", json!({"args":{}}), "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n");
    assert_eq!(status, 401); assert_eq!(persona["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
    let (status, guest) = request(release.port, "/auth/guest", json!({}), "");
    assert_eq!(status, 200);
    let (status, read) = request(release.port, "/q/all", json!({"args":{}}), &format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", guest["session"]["token"].as_str().unwrap()));
    assert_eq!(status, 200); assert_eq!(read["data"], json!([]), "refused seed never writes release data");
    assert!(banner.contains("snapback4 dev: development only — admits loopback personas and an unauthenticated /seed; deploy with snapback4 deploy + snapback4 serve, which refuse both."), "{banner}");
}

#[test]
fn dev_refuses_an_explicit_worker_path_instead_of_ignoring_it() {
    let dir = Directory::new();
    let out = dir.run(&["dev", "--worker", "custom.mjs"]);
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    assert!(out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(String::from_utf8(out.stderr).unwrap().contains("E_INPUT: dev takes no --worker"));
}

/// envelopes in one helper (002), shows the literal ready line (016 097), and
/// wraps the server lifecycle once (064 099); the platform binary names the npm
/// wrapper for `web-shell` instead of "unknown verb" (060).
#[test]
fn the_acceptance_recipe_reads_both_refusal_envelopes_and_web_shell_names_its_wrapper() {
    let guide = Command::new(BIN).args(["guide", "testing", "--section", "acceptance"]).output().unwrap();
    let guide = text(&guide);
    for term in ["snapback4/proof", "await startDev()", "await startStatic(\"dist\")", "PORT_LO..PORT_HI", "{state:'failed', why}", "HTTP acknowledgement is not React settlement"] {
        assert!(guide.contains(term), "guide testing's acceptance section names {term}:\n{guide}");
    }
    let output = Command::new(BIN).args(["web-shell", "dist"]).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(2));
    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
    assert!(stderr.contains("web-shell is the npm wrapper's verb") && stderr.contains("node_modules/.bin/snapback4 web-shell dist") && !stderr.contains("unknown verb"), "{stderr}");
}

#[test]
fn serve_checks_port_input_and_prints_its_actual_listener_and_ready_line() {
    let project = Project::new("use identity password\nquery alive():\n  return true\n");
    for bad in ["junk", "65536", "-1", "0-2", "20-10", "1-2-3", ""] {
        let output = project.run(&["serve", "missing-release", "--port", bad]);
        assert!(!output.status.success(), "{bad}: {}", text(&output));
        assert!(text(&output).contains("E_INPUT: serve --port"), "{bad}: {}", text(&output));
        assert!(!project.0.join("missing-release").exists(), "invalid input must not create a deployment");
    }
    let help = text(&project.run(&["rollback", "--help"]));
    assert!(help.contains("--to N") && help.contains("dormant per row") && help.contains("--check"), "{help}");
    let output = project.run(&["deploy", "deployment"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    let record: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(project.0.join("deployment/releases/1/device-compatibility.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(record["minimum_version"], "0.4.24");
    assert_eq!(record["server_version"], env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
    assert!(record["capabilities"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|c| c == "sync-generation-v1"));
    let held = std::net::TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)).unwrap();
    let taken = held.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    for port in ["0".to_owned(), format!("{taken}-{}", taken + 20)] {
        let child = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&project.0)
            .args(["serve", "deployment", &format!("--port={port}")])
            .stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
        let mut server = Dev(child);
        let stdout = server.0.stdout.take().unwrap();
        let (send, receive) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut line = String::new();
            let result = BufReader::new(stdout).read_line(&mut line).map(|_| line);
            let _ = send.send(result);
        });
        let line = receive.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)).expect("serve URL deadline").unwrap();
        let url = line.trim();
        assert!(url.starts_with("http://127.0.0.1:"), "{line:?}");
        let actual: u16 = url.rsplit(':').next().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_ne!(actual, 0);
        if port != "0" { assert!(actual > taken && actual <= taken + 20); }
        assert!(std::net::TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", actual)).is_err(), "the printed listener is held");
        let stderr = server.0.stderr.take().unwrap();
        let (send, receive) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut logs = BufReader::new(stderr);
            loop {
                let mut line = String::new();
                match logs.read_line(&mut line) {
                    Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                    Ok(_) if line.contains("SNAPBACK4_READY") => { let _ = send.send(line); break; }
                    _ => {},
                }
            }
        });
        let ready = receive.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(20)).expect("serve ready deadline");
        assert!(ready.contains("verb=serve") && ready.contains(&format!("url={url}")) && ready.contains(&format!("pid={}", server.0.id())), "{ready}");
        assert_eq!(http(url, "GET", "/schema", None, "").0, 401);
        drop(server);
    }
}

#[test]
fn test_uses_default_fixtures_and_explicit_fixture_overrides_them() {
    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  body: text <=20\n  public\nquery all():\n  return notes first 10 by .id\n");
    project.write("fixtures/notes.jsonl", "{\"id\":\"one\",\"body\":\"seeded\"}\n");
    project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", "{\"as\":\"alice\",\"op\":\"all\",\"count\":2}\n");
    let output = project.run(&["test"]);
    assert!(!output.status.success(), "the default case must fail: {}", text(&output));
    assert!(text(&output).contains("--help"));
}

#[test]
fn mutation_reply_omits_unrequested_id_and_keeps_result_id() {
    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(title: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes { title }\nmutation refuse():\n  require false else NO_PROOF\n  return null\n");
    let (_dev, url) = project.dev(0);
    for path in ["/m/add", "/m/refuse"] {
        let args = if path.ends_with("add") { r#"{"args":{"title":"first"}}"# } else { r#"{"args":{}}"# };
        let (_, reply) = http(&url, "POST", path, Some("alice"), args);
        assert!(!reply.as_object().unwrap().contains_key("id"), "{reply}");
        if path.ends_with("add") { assert!(reply["result"]["id"].is_string(), "{reply}"); }
    }
    let (_, sent) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
    assert_eq!(sent["id"], "proof-write");
    let (_, replay) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
    assert_eq!(replay["replayed"], true); assert!(replay.get("result").is_none());
}

#[test]
fn inspection_selects_release_explicitly_and_names_misplaced_sql_flags() {
    let project = Project::new("use identity password\ntable notes:\n  body: text <=100\n  public 'public notes'\n  online only\nquery alive():\n  return true\n");
    let deployed = project.run(&["deploy", "deployment"]);
    assert!(deployed.status.success(), "{}", text(&deployed));
    assert!(!project.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite").exists());
    for args in [&["sql", "SELECT id FROM notes"][..], &["data", "notes"]] {
        let output = project.run(args);
        assert!(!output.status.success());
        let error = text(&output);
        assert!(error.contains("--release <dir>") && error.contains("deployment"), "{error}");
        assert!(!project.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite").exists());
    }
    for flag in ["--release deployment", "--limit 10", "--json", "--json-objects"] {
        let output = project.run(&["sql", &format!("SELECT id FROM notes {flag}")]);
        assert!(!output.status.success());
        let error = text(&output);
        assert!(error.contains("inside the SQL argument") && error.contains("closing quote"), "{error}");
    }
    let output = project.run(&["sql", "SELECT '--release deployment' AS literal", "--release", "deployment", "--json-objects"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    let answer: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(answer["rows"][0]["literal"], "--release deployment");
    let output = project.run(&["data", "notes", "--release", "deployment"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    assert_eq!(output.stdout, b"");
    // Explicit release inspection also works outside an application's source tree.
    let output = Command::new(BIN).current_dir(std::env::temp_dir()).args(["data", "notes", "--release"])
        .arg(project.0.join("deployment")).output().unwrap();
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
}

/// The sweep consumes the store produced by ordered cases, including rows
/// absent from the initial seed, and its help explains both phases.
#[test]
fn rel3_sweep_runs_after_cases_and_derives_their_new_rows() {
    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(title: text <=80):\n  insert notes { title }\nquery one(id: notes):\n  return notes[id]\n");
    project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", "{\"op\":\"add\",\"args\":{\"title\":\"Ash\"},\"match\":{\"title\":\"Ash\"}}\n");
    let output = project.run(&["test"]);
    let text = format!("{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr));
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{text}");
    assert!(text.find("ok   test.cases.jsonl:1").unwrap() < text.find("test: operation (persona)").unwrap(), "{text}");
    let sweep = text.lines().find(|line| line.contains("one (alice)")).unwrap();
    assert!(sweep.contains("complete") && !sweep.contains("not run"), "{text}");
    let output = project.run(&["test", "--help"]);
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
    for term in ["then sweeps every query", "derives arguments", "Cases default to sent", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"] {
        assert!(text.contains(term), "{term}: {text}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn surf_saved_copy_hint_is_visible_without_turning_json_into_mixed_stdout() {
    let source="table notes:\n  body: text <=80\n  public\n  online only\n";
    let project=Project::new(source);
    project.write("snapback/old.q",source);
    for args in [&["check"][..],&["check","--json"][..]] {
        let output=project.run(args);
        assert!(!output.status.success());
        assert!(text(&output).contains("a saved copy? keep saved sources outside snapback/"),"{}",text(&output));
        if args.len()>1 { serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&output.stdout).unwrap(); }
    }
    project.write("snapback/old.q","table other:\n  body: text <=80\n  public\n  online only\n");
    let output=project.run(&["check"]);assert!(output.status.success(),"{}",text(&output));assert!(!text(&output).contains("a saved copy?"));
}

#[test]
fn surf_custom_entry_reference_is_not_labelled_an_unused_smoke_helper() {
    let project=Project::new("use identity password\n");
    project.write("src/native-smoke.ts","export function smokeSegment() {}\n");
    project.write("custom/entry.mjs","import '../src/native-smoke';\n");

    assert!(output.status.success(),"{}",text(&output));
    assert!(!text(&output).contains("info: src/native-smoke.ts"),"{}",text(&output));
}

#[test]
fn surf3_check_and_test_agree_on_relative_times_and_malformed_fixture_errors() {
    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\nquery clock(at: time, optional: time ?, times: [time] <=4):\n  return at\n");
    project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", "{\"op\":\"clock\",\"args\":{\"at\":\"now+2h\",\"optional\":\"now-1h\",\"times\":[\"now\",\"now+1ms\"]}}\n");
    let output = project.run(&["test"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    let output = project.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    assert!(!text(&output).contains("N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT"), "{}", text(&output));
    for time in ["now+wat", "now+0h", "now+9007199254740991w"] {
        project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", &format!("{{\"op\":\"clock\",\"args\":{{\"at\":\"{time}\",\"times\":[]}},\"expect\":\"E_INPUT\"}}\n"));
        for args in [&["test"][..], &["check", "--json"][..]] {
            let output = project.run(args);
            assert!(!output.status.success(), "{time}: {}", text(&output));
            assert!(text(&output).contains("E_FIXTURE_TIME"), "{}", text(&output));
            assert!(!text(&output).contains("N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT"), "{}", text(&output));
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn surf3_enum_assertions_do_not_shadow_aliases_and_match_set_checks_multiplicity() {
    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  state: enum('draft', 'published')\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(state: enum('draft', 'published')):\n  insert notes { state }\nquery all():\n  return notes first 10\n");
    project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", "{\"op\":\"add\",\"args\":{\"state\":\"draft\"},\"save\":\"draft\"}\n{\"op\":\"add\",\"args\":{\"state\":\"published\"}}\n{\"op\":\"all\",\"matchSet\":[{\"state\":\"published\"},{\"state\":\"draft\",\"id\":\"$draft\"}]}\n");
    let output = project.run(&["test"]);
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
    project.write("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl", "{\"op\":\"add\",\"args\":{\"state\":\"draft\"},\"save\":\"draft\",\"match\":{\"state\":\"draft\"}}\n{\"op\":\"all\",\"matchSet\":[{},{}]}\n");
    let output = project.run(&["test"]);
    assert!(!output.status.success() && text(&output).contains("matchSet differs"), "{}", text(&output));
}

test('browser session authority commits through IndexedDB before releasing the session lock', { timeout: hangBudget(164) }, async t => {
  const project = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sb4-session-authority-'));
  const sourceDir = join(project, 'snapback'); mkdirSync(sourceDir);
  writeFileSync(join(sourceDir, 'schema.q'), `use identity password

table audit:
  total: int
  public 'Non-idempotent session admission audit.'
  update <- allow

table notes:
  owner: principal
  body: text <=200
  by byOwner: owner, id
  read <- .owner = viewer
  sync to .owner

query privateNotes():
  return notes[viewer] first 100 by byOwner

mutation click():
  row = audit['audit'] ! else NOT_FOUND
  update audit[row] { total: row.total + 1 }

query total():
  return audit['audit'].total
`);
  t.after(() => rmSync(project, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const path = name => join(root, 'packages/snapback4/src', name);
  const mutant = process.env.SNAPBACK4_SESSION_AUTHORITY_MUTANT, sourceOverrides = {};
  const replace = (file, before, after) => {
    const source = readFileSync(path(file), 'utf8'); assert.ok(source.includes(before), `mutant ${mutant} must match`);
    sourceOverrides[path(file)] = source.replace(before, after);
  };
  if (mutant === 'mirror') replace('admission-authority.ts', 'const raw = await context.read(stamp.key);', 'const raw = localStorage.getItem(stamp.key);');
  if (mutant === 'commit') replace('session-authority.ts', 'try { await committed; }', 'try { void committed; }');
  if (mutant === 'source') replace('admission-authority.ts', '!stamp.source || stamp.source !== current.source || ', '');
  if (mutant === 'ownership') replace('admission-authority.ts', 'else if (browserAdmission()) throw new StorageWarning(', 'else if (false) throw new StorageWarning(');
  if (mutant === 'refusal-fence') replace('web-journal.ts', 'if (!accepted) authority.recheck();', '/* mutant: retain the stale scope */');
  if (mutant === 'mirror-repair') replace('session-authority.ts', 'try { updateMirror(key, value); }', 'try { /* mutant: retain speculative mirror */ }');
  if (mutant === 'epoch-migration') replace('app.ts', 'await authority?.reconcileEpoch(sessionKey(url));', '/* mutant: discard the legacy epoch */');
  if (mutant === 'absence') replace('session-authority.ts', 'const value = (request.result ?? null) as string | null;', 'if (request.result === undefined) return null;\n      const value = (request.result ?? null) as string | null;');
  if (mutant === 'epoch-allocation') replace('session-authority.ts', 'if (BigInt(legacy) > BigInt(authoritative)) await write(epochKey, legacy, before);', 'if (true) await write(epochKey, BigInt(legacy) > BigInt(authoritative) ? legacy : authoritative, before);');
  if (mutant === 'higher-mirror') replace('session-authority.ts', 'if (BigInt(legacy) > BigInt(authoritative))', 'if (marker === "0" && BigInt(legacy) > BigInt(authoritative))');
  const witness = await launch({ sourceDir, seed: [{ table: 'audit', row: { id: 'audit', total: 0 } }], sourceOverrides, entrySource: `
import { createApp } from '${path('app.ts')}';
import { keepSession, localStorageSessionStore, sessionKey } from '${path('auth.ts')}';
import { mintId } from '${path('client.ts')}';
Object.assign(window, { createApp, keepSession, localStorageSessionStore, sessionKey, mintId });
window.clickOp = { kind: 'mutation', name: 'click' };
window.boot = async ({ url, session, holdAdmission = false, strippedDevice = false }) => {
  if (session) await keepSession(url, session, localStorageSessionStore());
  window.sent = []; window.lockDepth = 0; window.transactions = []; window.holdAdmission = holdAdmission;
  const request = navigator.locks.request.bind(navigator.locks);
  navigator.locks.request = (name, ...args) => {
    const callback = args.pop();
    return request(name, ...args, async lock => {
      if (name === sessionKey(url)) window.lockDepth++;
      try { return await callback(lock); }
      finally { if (name === sessionKey(url)) window.lockDepth--; }
    });
  };
  const transaction = IDBDatabase.prototype.transaction;
  IDBDatabase.prototype.transaction = function (...args) {
    const tx = transaction.apply(this, args);
    if (this.name === 'snapback4-session-authority' && args[1] === 'readwrite') {
      const entry = { strict: args[2]?.durability === 'strict', started: lockDepth };
      transactions.push(entry);
      tx.addEventListener('complete', () => entry.completed = lockDepth);
      tx.addEventListener('abort', () => entry.aborted = lockDepth);
    }
    return tx;
  };
  const persist = indexedDbPersistence(), save = persist.save;
  persist.save = async (name, changes) => {
    if (window.holdAdmission && changes.some(c => c.s === 'o' && c.v !== null)) {
      window.admissionHeld = true;
      await new Promise(resolve => window.releaseAdmission = () => { window.holdAdmission = false; resolve(); });

      const core = await opener.open(...args); window.core = core;
      // A method-only adapter cannot silently turn an app into a raw client.
      return strippedDevice ? { call: core.call, close: core.close } : { ...core };
    } },
    fetch: async (input, init) => {
      const route = new URL(String(input)).pathname;
      if (route === '/changes') {
        window.pollHeld = true;
        await new Promise((_, reject) => {
          if (init.signal.aborted) reject(init.signal.reason);
          else init.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(init.signal.reason), { once: true });
        });
      }
      // Keep the old bearer valid at the backend: only local authority can
      // prevent the stale click, so server revocation cannot mask a failure.
      if (route === '/auth/logout') throw new TypeError('revocation is offline');
      if (route.startsWith('/m/')) window.sent.push(JSON.parse(init.body).id);
      return fetch(input, init);
    } });
  return app.ready;
};
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.pollHeld);
    const evidence = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      await app.client.sync();
      const get = IDBObjectStore.prototype.get; let aborted = 0;
      IDBObjectStore.prototype.get = function (...args) {
        const request = get.apply(this, args), tx = this.transaction;
        if (tx.db.name === 'snapback4-session-authority' && tx.mode === 'readonly') {
          // Refuse every authority read during this query: a concurrent poll
          // may own the first transaction, independently of the query's read.
          request.addEventListener('success', () => { aborted = lockDepth; try { tx.abort(); } catch {} });
        }
        return request;
      };
      let read;
      try { read = await app.client.query({ kind: 'query', name: 'total' }, {}); }
      finally { IDBObjectStore.prototype.get = get; }
      await app.close(); return { read, aborted };
    });
    assert.ok(evidence.aborted > 0);
    assert.equal(evidence.read.denied?.code, 'E_CLIENT');
    assert.equal('data' in evidence.read, false);
  });

  await t.test('a peer logout commits before the next lock holder and refuses a click captured from a stale mirror', async () => {

    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => old.acquired), true);

    const peer = await page.context().newPage();

      assert.ok(JSON.parse(authoritative).snapback4Logout, 'next holder must see the committed tombstone');
      const tx = await peer.evaluate(() => transactions);
      assert.ok(tx.length > 0 && tx.every(tx => tx.strict && tx.started > 0 && tx.completed > 0), JSON.stringify(tx));
      await peer.evaluate(url => new Promise(resolve => {
        void navigator.locks.request(sessionKey(url), async () => { resolve(); await new Promise(release => window.releaseLock = release); });
      }), url);
      const captured = await page.evaluate(({ url, mirror }) => {
        // Chromium's per-renderer cache may remain stale across the lock. Inject
        // exactly that read fault, not an authoritative write or a timing delay.
        const get = Storage.prototype.getItem, key = sessionKey(url);
        Storage.prototype.getItem = function (k) { return k === key ? mirror : k === key + '.epoch' ? null : get.call(this, k); };
        window.restoreMirror = () => Storage.prototype.getItem = get;
        return capture();
      }, { url, mirror });

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "../tests/serve_poison/mod.rs"]
mod poison_tests;

// All raw (asset/native/route/empty) replies advertise the admitted generation too.
pub(crate) fn generation_header()->Option<Header>{
    crate::release_requests::reply_generation().map(|g|Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Generation",g.to_string()).unwrap())
}

// Typed mutation inputs have one terminal failure envelope, including wire decoding.
fn argument_refusal(request:Request,shared:&Shared,id:Option<&str>,why:Refusal){
    if !request.url().starts_with("/m/") {return operation_refusal(request,shared,400,why);}
    match shared.read_identity() {
        Ok((store_id,head,generation))=>{
            let mut reply=json!({"state":"failed","why":refusal_json(&why),"store_id":store_id,"head":head,"generation":generation});
            if let Some(id)=id {reply["id"]=json!(id);}
            respond_timed(request,shared,200,reply);

snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/auth.rs
snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_identity.rs
snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/dev_clock_store.rs
import { hangBudget, waitFor } from '../packages/snapback4/test/helpers/wait.mjs';
// What every Chromium witness needs: the local-first client bundled into a
// page, a seeded `snapback4 dev`, and a browser with a CDP session for the
// heap. Each witness seeds its own rows and measures its own thing.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { mkdtempSync, cpSync, rmSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, globSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

export const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

/** The binary under test: `SNAPBACK4_BIN` (a release build for throughput numbers), else the debug build. */
export const binary = () => process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ? resolve(process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN) : join(root, "target/debug/snapback4");

export function chromiumExecutable() {
  const candidates = [
    ...globSync(`${process.env.HOME}/Library/Caches/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-mac-arm64/Google Chrome for Testing.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome for Testing`).sort().reverse(),
    ...globSync(`${process.env.HOME}/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-mac-arm64/Google Chrome for Testing.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome for Testing`).sort().reverse(),
    ...globSync(`${process.env.HOME}/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-linux64/chrome`).sort().reverse(),
    ...globSync(`${process.env.HOME}/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-linux/chrome`).sort().reverse(),
    "/usr/bin/google-chrome",
    "/usr/bin/chromium",
    "/usr/bin/chromium-browser",
    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
    "/Applications/Chromium.app/Contents/MacOS/Chromium",
  ];
  const exe = candidates.find((path) => existsSync(path));
  if (!exe) throw new Error("no Chromium: install playwright's chromium or Google Chrome");
  return exe;
}

/** The chat backend's scale seed: `groups` conversations of `messages`
 * each, `members` principals in every one (alice admin, bob member, then
 * fillers), so bob's partition is the whole thing. */
export function scaleSeed({ groups, messages, members }) {
  const rows = [];
  const fillers = Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, members - 2) }, (_, n) => `dev:member${n}`);
  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    const conversation = `c${String(g).padStart(3, "0")}`;
    rows.push({ table: "conversations", row: { id: conversation, title: `Group ${g}`, creatorId: "dev:alice" } });
    for (const [n, principal] of ["dev:alice", "dev:bob", ...fillers].entries()) {
      rows.push({ table: "memberships", row: { id: `${conversation}-m${n}`, conversationId: conversation, principal, role: n === 0 ? "admin" : "member", joinedAt: n } });
    }
    for (let m = 0; m < messages; m++) {
      rows.push({ table: "messages", row: { id: `${conversation}-msg${String(m).padStart(5, "0")}`, conversationId: conversation, authorId: m % 2 ? "dev:alice" : "dev:bob", body: `message ${m} in group ${g}: the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog`, at: 1_700_000_000_000 + g * 1_000_000 + m, editedAt: null, photo: null } });
    }
  }
  return rows;
}

/** A `snapback4 dev` on the chat backend in a temporary project, seeded. */
export async function devServer({ seed, extraSource = "", sourceDir, port = 0, clockOffset, watch = false }) {
  const app = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "sb4-witness-app-"));
  cpSync(sourceDir ?? join(root, "apps/chat/snapback"), join(app, "snapback"), { recursive: true, filter: (path) => !/\/worker\.(ts|mjs)$/.test(path) });
  rmSync(join(app, "snapback/generated"), { recursive: true, force: true });
  if (extraSource) writeFileSync(join(app, "snapback/media.q"), extraSource);
  const server = spawn(binary(), ["dev", "--port", String(port), ...(watch ? [] : ["--no-watch"]), ...(clockOffset ? ["--clock-offset", clockOffset] : [])], { cwd: app, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"] });
  let output = "", log = "", spawnError;
  server.stdout.on("data", data => { output += data; });
  server.stderr.on("data", data => { log += data; });
  server.on("error", error => { spawnError = error; });
  const closed = new Promise(resolve => server.once("close", resolve));
  let closing;
  const close = () => closing ??= (async () => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { if (server.exitCode == null && server.signalCode == null) server.kill("SIGKILL"); }, 5000);
    try {
      if (server.exitCode == null && server.signalCode == null) server.kill();
      await closed;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      rmSync(app, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  })();
  try {
    const api = await waitFor(() => {
      if (spawnError) throw spawnError;
      if (server.exitCode != null || server.signalCode != null) throw new Error(`dev exited before readiness: ${log}`);
      return output.match(/^http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d+\r?\n/m)?.[0].trim();
    }, 'witness dev readiness', { diagnostics: () => log });
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const seeded = await fetch(`${api}/seed`, { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(seed) }).then(r => r.json());
    const seedMs = +(performance.now() - t0).toFixed(1);
    return { api, app, pid: server.pid, seeded: seeded.seeded, seedMs, close };
  } catch (error) {
    await close();
    throw error;
  }
}

/** Bundle the client into a page, start a seeded dev server, open Chromium. */
export async function launch({ seed, extraSource = "", headless = true, sourceDir, entrySource = "", sourceOverrides = {}, clockOffset, worker = false, watch = false }) {
  const require = createRequire(join(root, "package.json"));
  const { chromium } = require("playwright-core");
  const exe = chromiumExecutable();
  const { build } = await import(pathToFileURL(createRequire(join(root, "apps/chat/package.json")).resolve("vite")).href);
  // The page: the one client over the wasm device, bundled with the wasm
  // beside it (`scripts/build-wasm.sh` first), served from a directory.
  const harness = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "sb4-witness-"));
  const entry = join(harness, "harness.ts");
  // Keep defaults in their own module: custom entries may import the same
  // public names without redeclaring bootstrap bindings in one module.
  writeFileSync(join(harness, "bootstrap.ts"), `import { createLocalClient } from "${join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/local.ts")}";\nimport { webDevice, indexedDbPersistence } from "${join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/web.ts")}";\nimport { WebDevice } from "${join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/wasm/snapback4_device.js")}";\nimport { createApp } from "${join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/app.ts")}";\nimport { localStorageSessionStore } from "${join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/auth.ts")}";\nObject.assign(window, { webDevice, indexedDbPersistence, WebDevice, createApp, localStorageSessionStore });\n(window as any).createLocalClient = (options: any) => createLocalClient({ device: webDevice({ worker: ${worker} }), ...options });\n`);
  writeFileSync(entry, `import "./bootstrap.ts";\n${entrySource}\n`);
  writeFileSync(join(harness, "index.html"), `<!doctype html><title>witness</title><script type="module" src="./harness.ts"></script>`);
  const overrides = () => ({ name: "witness-source-overrides", enforce: "pre", transform(code, id) { return sourceOverrides[id] ? { code: sourceOverrides[id], map: null } : null; } });
  await build({ root: harness, resolve: { alias: { "snapback4/contract": join(root, "packages/snapback4/src/contract.ts") } }, logLevel: "silent", plugins: [overrides()], worker: { plugins: () => [overrides()] }, build: { outDir: join(harness, "dist"), emptyOutDir: true, minify: false, target: "es2022", assetsInlineLimit: 0 } });

  const dev = await devServer({ seed, extraSource, sourceDir, clockOffset, watch });
  const { api, app, seeded, seedMs } = dev;

  const types = { ".html": "text/html", ".js": "text/javascript", ".wasm": "application/wasm" };
  const pageServer = createServer((req, res) => {
    const path = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, "http://127.0.0.1").pathname);
    const file = join(harness, "dist", path === "/" ? "index.html" : path);
    if (!existsSync(file)) { res.statusCode = 404; res.end(); return; }
    res.setHeader("content-type", types[file.slice(file.lastIndexOf("."))] ?? "application/octet-stream");
    res.end(readFileSync(file));
  });
  await new Promise((r) => pageServer.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
  const pageUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${pageServer.address().port}/`;
  let browser;
  try {
  browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: exe, headless, timeout: hangBudget() });
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  context.setDefaultTimeout(hangBudget()); context.setDefaultNavigationTimeout(hangBudget());
  const page = await context.newPage();
  const cdp = await context.newCDPSession(page);
  await cdp.send("Performance.enable");
  const ready = async () => { await page.goto(pageUrl); await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.createLocalClient === "function"); };
  await ready();
  return {
    page,
    api,
    app,
    seeded,
    seedMs,
    chromium: exe.includes("Testing") ? exe.split("/").find((p) => p.startsWith("chromium-")) : "system chrome",
    async reload() { await page.reload(); await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.createLocalClient === "function"); },
    async heapMb() { const { metrics } = await cdp.send("Performance.getMetrics"); return +((metrics.find((m) => m.name === "JSHeapUsedSize")?.value ?? 0) / 1_048_576).toFixed(1); },
    async close() {
      await browser.close();
      pageServer.close();
      rmSync(harness, { recursive: true, force: true });
      await dev.close();
    },
  };
  } catch (error) {
    await browser?.close();
    pageServer.close();
    await dev.close();
    rmSync(harness, { recursive: true, force: true });
    throw error;
  }
}

export function record(line) {
  const path = join(root, "metrics/witness.jsonl");
  writeFileSync(path, `${readFileSync(path, "utf8")}${JSON.stringify(line)}\n`);
}

# LLP 3000.011.003.000.000: Packaged application proof kit

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Implemented
**Systems:** Snapback 4 proof tooling, starters, fixtures, release source selection
**Author:** Codex for Charlie Cheever
**Date:** 2026-10-14
**Revised:** 2026-10-17 (KIT3 proof and clock contracts); 2026-10-16 (REG missing frontend export diagnostic; §11: wave-14 DAY4); 2026-10-16 (§9: wave-13 DAY3; §10: wave-13 KIT2); 2026-10-16 (§8: explicit platform boundary and client-source guard); 2026-10-15 (§8: wave-12 KIT and observational listener qualification)
**Related:** [Starters](./3000.011.003.000-offline-ready-application-starters.rfc.md), [Release activation](./3000.000.000-retained-storage-and-hot-release.rfc.md), [Built application proof](./3000.011.005.000-qualify-the-built-application.rfc.md)

STAGE staging and persistent startDev defaults are amended by [LLP 3000.000.000.001](./3000.000.000.001-staging-with-real-users.rfc.md); KIT4 owns other ongoing helper changes.

## 1. Contract

Implementer: PROOF workstream, 2026-10-14. Wave 11 fix list 1 authorizes
one published `snapback4/proof` Node module. It owns orchestration, never client
custody or backend/bundler supervision in the product. Existing `startDev(args)`
and `stopChild(child | {url, stop})` recipes remain usable as module exports.

`startDev`, `startServe(directory)`, `startStatic(directory)` and
`startVite(directory)` return `{url, pid, logs, stop}`. Options carry cwd,
environment, arguments and bounded ready/stop deadlines. Read PORT_LO/PORT_HI
at invocation: absent means an OS-assigned port; exactly one set refuses. With
both set, validate 1 <= LO <= HI <= 65535 and bind the first free port in that
inclusive range; exhaustion refuses. Qualification's occupied first port must
be skipped, never adopted. The existing native SNAPBACK4_READY line already names its direct child PID (LLP 3000.011.004.000); no new readiness log is needed. The listening process binds first and keeps that socket;
never adopt an already-serving port. Vite uses its supported JavaScript server
API in a directly spawned Node child, strict ports, the project's config and
a per-launch identity middleware. The directly spawned frontend calls its
server listen API for each candidate; no probe-and-release step exists. With
no range it calls listen on port 0, which the tested Vite API supports with
strictPort; it reads the actual retained socket address. Static hosting uses a directly spawned Node
child with traversal protection, correct wasm MIME and SPA fallback.

All child stdout/stderr go to separate files beneath `logs/`. Read complete
lines from those files, strip ANSI, bound readiness, and reject premature exit.
Native ready lines must name the directly spawned PID. To assert HTTP identity,
the kit sets a random SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID only in the child environment;
`/auth/config` includes `proof_id` only when this variable is nonempty. It
confers no authority or bypass, and ordinary servers omit it. Both static and Vite frontend child
responses carry an `x-snapback4-proof` marker. Identity validation
precedes returning a handle. No public development door is enabled in serve.

Register every spawned child and browser process for an exit-time SIGKILL
sweep, using retained native ChildProcess handles, not process-name kills.
TERM followed by bounded KILL/reap stops a child. Every cleanup action has
its own bound and later actions still run when an earlier action fails.
`cli(args)` runs the native binary, awaits close, returns separate stdout and
stderr, and has a command deadline. It never runs npm/npx or shell strings.

`withBrowser({executablePath: process.env.CHROME}, callback)` launches Chromium
through playwright-core, closes with a bound, and registers its actual browser
process. Root needs --no-sandbox; other hosts first attempt a sandboxed launch
and retry without it only on recognized sandbox-unavailable diagnostics: "No usable sandbox", "Running as
root without --no-sandbox", "Failed to move to new namespace", "SUID sandbox",
"sandbox ... Operation not permitted", or "Failed to unshare ... Operation not
permitted". Unrelated failures never retry.
`waitForFresh` polls for nonempty changed text; `fillRN` clears and types real
keystrokes; `statusText` uses text selectors; `selectByValue` selects stable
option values. The browser executable is CHROME (when provided), undefined executablePath is omitted from launch options, then Playwright's
installed Chromium is used; an absent executable refuses at launch, with its path.
waitForFresh defaults to 30 seconds. Binary resolution is resolveBinary from
the same installed snapback4 package (override SNAPBACK4_BIN only for explicit
workspace tests); the opt-in identity requires CLI/client 0.4.28 or newer and
an older CLI omits proof_id and therefore fails the exact marker assertion; no inferred version comparison or success claim is made. cli(args) does not
invoke package managers; publication uses its existing guarded command runner.

None interprets an HTTP acknowledgement as rendered settlement.

## 2. Explicit source and aliases

`deploy dir --schema file` compiles exactly that saved Quarry file and stages
those same bytes as canonical snapback/schema.q, preserving operation names
regardless of the saved file's name. Compile diagnostics name the selected input.
This happens without swapping the working schema or generating its types.
It does not concatenate other snapback/*.q files. It allocates ordinary numeric
immutable releases, uses the same .pending-adoption marker, transaction/history,
latest selection, restoration-intent preview and no-op checks as normal deploy;
no alternate activation path or restoration bypass exists. The normal deployment path
retains multi-file behavior and activation semantics. Server/native companion source comes from the current project, while all
schema-dependent generated signatures and native builds run against the selected
schema in a private snapshot. A uniquely created snapshot project has
snapback/schema.q, captured companion source, fresh snapback/generated/
and copied native sources (target caches excluded). Host-installed node_modules
is linked into the private project; package.json preserves host module policy.
The snapshot is RAII-owned and removed on success or error; nothing is executed
through the working generated types. Its server entry is typechecked against
the fresh generated server signature before activation; its native crate builds
against fresh generated native.rs into its own native-target directory. Never reuse the working generated signature or
write selected-schema types there. Package prepared signatures and native bytes
with the captured saved source; retain the original host package installation
for dependency resolution. Standalone means no Project::locate result from the current directory/ancestors; that saved source has no companion app. Native snapshot manifests preserve their source layout and rebase only Cargo
dependency paths against the original native crate's directory. TOML parsing
covers normal, build/dev, target-specific, patch/replace and local workspace
dependency maps. Absolute paths stay equivalent; missing dependencies refuse
before compilation. Never rewrite the package's own lib/bin paths to the
working tree; those must compile against the selected private generated types.
 Missing/stale external server
import graphs fail private checking with an actionable TypeScript diagnostic. Keep saved variants outside snapback/ to avoid normal
multi-file compilation. Rollback selects an existing immutable release, never
reads the current schema. The proof `deploy` helper requires a schema path;
`rollback` requires an explicit target; `testSource` requires a source path.
`test --source file` uses the selected bytes in a memory engine (optionally
with fixtures), so it cannot adopt a working store. Diagnostics name the file.

Fixture alias arguments and assertions follow [REL3 §5](./3000.000.000.000-release-spanning-clients-and-development-contracts.rfc.md#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence). Protocol request decoding never sees this saved-name map and accepts all ordinary literal data unchanged. Saved aliases remain explicit `$name` / `$name.field`. In saved-value argument positions (typed by fixture contracts §7), a bare string
matching an earlier save alias refuses with the alias name and `$name` rewrite
before wire decoding. Text/enum/JSON values and assertion strings stay literal. Unknown `$name` refuses by exact name lookup, never fuzzy matching.
`$name.field` requires every field in the saved object, refusing by the missing
field's name (a non-record has no fields). A `$` appearing inside ordinary text
is literal. `$$text` escapes to the literal `$text`; `{"$literal": value}`
in a fixture argument escapes any value, including an exact bare alias name. This affects only fixture case argument substitution, not protocol decoding.
Fixture preflight/sweep excludes explicitly saved arguments as before.

The already-shipped ?api= override is not introduced or expanded here. Proofs
use it on an owned loopback frontend and fresh browser context; production URL
policy belongs to the existing client/starter surface. The user explicitly
assigns source flags, fixture aliases, frontend proofs and their manifest entries
to PROOF; the two lane-owned text areas below remain separate.
